<div class="fleet-caps-key"><span><i class="e"></i>present</span><span><i class="p"></i>partial, as its note explains</span><span><i class="m"></i>missing</span><span><i class="na"></i>does not apply to this site</span></div>
<details class="fleet-site-notes">
<summary>Feature criteria and the evidence for each verdict</summary>
<h4>Baseline audit</h4><p>Each Mech&#x27;s live GitHub Pages site as checked on October 25, 2026 (US Pacific time), independently of the repository pins used by the census. One AI reviewer per site reassessed the 24 earlier catalogue features from 18:00 to 21:29 UTC in an isolated headless Chrome profile at 390 and 1,280 px: identifier and label searches, filters, URL restoration, theme persistence across pages, horizontal overflow and deliberately aborted data requests where applicable. Corpus-wide static audits of the committed or deployed pages supplied the corpus-wide counts; notes name the sampled routes rather than claiming an exhaustive check of every record. A separate pass rated the ten newly catalogued features on all fourteen sites from 21:37 to 22:20 UTC. A second reviewer then independently re-tested every changed and every new verdict live from 01:03 to 02:08 UTC on October 26 (October 25, US Pacific time), and an arbiter settled each disagreement; the evidence-link counts for DUFMech, PathwayMech and TraitMech were then measured on their main commits 0b3ed0f, 537313d and 5f630a4. Each deployed_revision is the deployment, verified as successful, that the site&#x27;s review of the 24 earlier features observed. TraitMech redeployed dcafc9d at 18:28 UTC during its review, and only its keyboard pass ran on that curation-only deployment. AntibioticMech, CellStructureMech and TraitMech had redeployed before the new-feature pass began, and NaturalProductMech redeployed at 22:18 UTC during it, adding review files under reviews/ but changing no page. The new-feature and re-test passes rated the live sites, some redeployed since the recorded revisions; later deployments may differ.</p>
<h4>Features</h4><dl><dt>Search</dt><dd>A labeled text search over the whole published corpus, tested with a known identifier and a known label. Present: it finds records by identifier and by label, shows how many match, and every match can be opened. Partial: results are capped with no route to the rest, no match count is shown, identifiers are not searchable, or search works only within one category.</dd><dt>Filters</dt><dd>Filter controls on record properties, with a way to clear them. Present: at least one filter on a record property in the main record listing, with a reset. Partial: filters only on a map or plot page, or a single selector without a reset. A free-text box is a search, not a filter.</dd><dt>Result counts</dt><dd>A count of matching records that updates with search and filters, distinct from the total. Partial: only a static total.</dd><dt>Sorting</dt><dd>The reader can change the sort order of a listing. Present: a sort control or sortable column headings.</dd><dt>Linkable search</dt><dd>Search and filter state is kept in the URL, so a filtered view can be bookmarked and restores on load. Partial: some state, such as a facet or a selected record, restores from the URL but the main search does not. Does not apply: the site has no search or filters.</dd><dt>Complete catalog</dt><dd>Every record the site counts is reachable from it, by listing, pagination, shards or lazy loading. Partial: some records the site counts cannot be reached from its pages.</dd><dt>Search from any page</dt><dd>The landing page and record pages carry a search box that opens the main listing with the query applied. Present: both the landing page and record pages hand off a query that the listing restores and applies. Partial: only one of them, or the query arrives but is not applied. Does not apply: the site has no listing search.</dd><dt>Record pages</dt><dd>Each record has its own stable, linkable URL.</dd><dt>Term links</dt><dd>Ontology terms show a readable label beside the CURIE, and the CURIE links to a resolver. Present: the record&#x27;s own grounding and the terms in its main sections are labeled and linked; a few minor prefixes may be plain text. Partial: a whole main section of terms is unlinked, unlabeled or broken, or the record&#x27;s own identifier does not resolve for many records.</dd><dt>Evidence links</dt><dd>References (PMID, DOI or URL) shown on record pages are linked. Partial: references are plain text, or only a minority of the records that cite something link it. A reference counts as linked when its record page links it; an identifier shown inside or beside a linked citation is not a plain-text reference, and a few records with a stray unlinked mention do not make the feature partial.</dd><dt>Curation status</dt><dd>Each record shows its curation or review status.</dd><dt>Curation history</dt><dd>The record view shows its curation events: what changed, when and by whom.</dd><dt>Source link</dt><dd>The record view links to the record&#x27;s own source file in the GitHub repository. Partial: the file is named as plain text, or only the repository root is linked.</dd><dt>Domain visual</dt><dd>A view suited to the domain, such as a structure or embedding map, a pathway or causal diagram, an interaction network or a lineage. Partial: the visual exists but is broken for readers.</dd><dt>Cross-Mech links</dt><dd>Record views link to specific records in sibling Mechs or in kg-microbe. Partial: related records are named as plain text, or the links are broken.</dd><dt>Text embedding map</dt><dd>A map of the corpus projected from text embeddings (the fleet&#x27;s shared embedding sets), with search and filters, where clicking a point opens its record. Present: the map renders, plots every record the site counts or states which are left out, and point clicks open the right record. Partial: renders but clicks fail, records are silently missing, or it does not render for some readers. Missing: a map is published but renders for no reader. Does not apply: no text map is published.</dd><dt>Open questions</dt><dd>Record pages show the record&#x27;s open questions or knowledge gaps with their status, and an index links them across the corpus. Present: records that carry discussions show them and a linked index lists them. Partial: shown on records but the index is unlinked or empty, or the index exists without record display. Does not apply: no record carries discussions.</dd><dt>Curation notice</dt><dd>The site says how its records were produced (for example AI-assisted curation or import) and what each curation status means, including that a workflow status is not a human review where that is the case. Present: stated on the landing page or footer and explained beside the status on record pages. Partial: one of the two only.</dd><dt>Report a problem</dt><dd>Each record page offers a route to report an error or suggest an edit, such as a prefilled GitHub issue or an edit link for the record&#x27;s file. Present: every record page has a working route. Partial: a site-wide route only, or one that does not identify the record.</dd><dt>Corpus statistics</dt><dd>The landing page shows generated corpus counts.</dd><dt>Category browsing</dt><dd>Category or class pages lead into subsets of the corpus. Partial: categories are reachable only as a filter, or their index is not linked from the landing page.</dd><dt>Suite link</dt><dd>A visible link to the X-Mech Suite directory at culturebotai.github.io/mechs/.</dd><dt>Breadcrumbs</dt><dd>Record and subset pages show a linked trail back to the listing and the record&#x27;s category or parent. Present: every record page has a trail whose links resolve. Partial: some page types only, or links that do not resolve.</dd><dt>Not-found page</dt><dd>A missing URL anywhere on the site returns HTTP 404 with the site&#x27;s own page, offering navigation or search back into the site. Present: missing URLs at the site root and under its page directories get the themed page with working recovery links. Partial: only some paths, or the page lacks the theme or recovery links. Missing: GitHub&#x27;s generic 404.</dd><dt>Downloads</dt><dd>A bulk machine-readable export of all records is linked from the site. Partial: only the repository, per-record files, partial exports, or a bulk file that no page links. Missing: no data or repository link at all.</dd><dt>Schema docs</dt><dd>LinkML schema documentation is published on or linked from the site. Partial: the site says a schema exists but links only the repository root, or a documentation page exists that nothing links. Does not apply: there is no schema.</dd><dt>License</dt><dd>The site states the data and code licenses, consistent with the repository&#x27;s license files. Partial: stated on only some pages, or inconsistent with the repository.</dd><dt>Build or version</dt><dd>The site states when it was built, which commit it shows, or a dated version of its own records. Partial: only the dates or releases of upstream sources.</dd><dt>Theme toggle</dt><dd>A light and dark toggle whose choice carries across pages. Partial: follows the operating system&#x27;s setting without a toggle, or some linked pages ignore the choice.</dd><dt>Narrow screens</dt><dd>No horizontal page overflow at 390 pixels on pages linked from the main navigation, the browse page or record pages; wide tables scroll in their own region. Partial: some of those pages overflow.</dd><dt>Load errors</dt><dd>When a data request fails, the page shows an error rather than an empty or misleading result. Partial: some failures are silent or misleading. Does not apply: the pages fetch no data.</dd><dt>Skip link</dt><dd>The first keyboard stop on each page is a link that skips to the main content. Present: landing, listing and record pages each start with a skip link whose target exists in main. Partial: some page types only.</dd><dt>Text alternative for visuals</dt><dd>Every map, diagram or graph has a table or list alternative a keyboard or screen-reader user can reach, listing what the visual shows with record links. Present: every visual on the site has one. Partial: some visuals only. A link to the general listing does not count: the alternative must list what that visual shows. Does not apply: the site has no maps, diagrams or graphs.</dd><dt>Works without JavaScript</dt><dd>With JavaScript disabled, the listing still reaches every record and record pages still show their content, or the page says plainly that it needs JavaScript. Present: listing and record content render without JavaScript. Partial: record pages render but the listing is empty, or a page fails silently. Missing: blank pages with no message.</dd></dl>
<h4><a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/index.html">HabitatMech</a></h4><ul><li><b>Search</b> <span>partial</span>: Browse now searches all 3,209 records by identifier and label: ENVO:03000033, habitatmech:BACDIVE.38260eea00 and BTO:0003167 each give one match, and marine sediment gives three that open. But it shows at most 200 hits with no route to the rest, so marine reports 217 and lists 200, and on a throttled connection the first query showed the count for its first keystroke. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/browse.html?q=ENVO%3A03000033">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Filters</b> <span>present</span>: Browse has a labeled Grounding status select with Reset filters, and category pages add a Source vocabulary select: EXACT gives 1,054, sediment + EXACT 13, and marine + EXACT + GOLD on Aquatic 46. Reset and Clear restore the unfiltered view and clear the URL. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/browse.html?grounding=EXACT&amp;q=sediment">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Result counts</b> <span>present</span>: Live counts update with search and filters: Browse shows 13 of 13 matching habitats for sediment + EXACT, a category page 89 matching records of the 468 in Aquatic, and the map wrapper 139 matching records. Under throttling, the first Browse query showed the count for its first keystroke until the next input. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/category/aquatic.html?q=sediment">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Sorting</b> <span>present</span>: Labeled Sort order selects on Browse and category pages offer five orders, by assertions, name and grounding, and every order checked on 140-, 200- and 468-row listings had no violation. The sort is kept in ?sort= and combines with filters; column headings are not sortable. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/category/aquatic.html?sort=label-desc">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Linkable search</b> <span>present</span>: Browse keeps the query, grounding and sort in the URL, and category pages add the source; a sediment, EXACT, Z-A Browse view and a marine, EXACT, GOLD, Z-A Aquatic view restore every control and their 13 and 46 results in a fresh browser and after reload. The map wrapper keeps ?q=. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/browse.html?q=sediment&amp;grounding=EXACT&amp;sort=name-desc">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Complete catalog</b> <span>present</span>: The 16 linked category listing pages link exactly 3,209 record pages, equal to the category indexes, the landing total, the semantic map&#x27;s points and the sitemap, and all 3,209 returned 200 in the 390 px sweep. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/browse.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Search from any page</b> <span>present</span>: The landing hero and record top-bar searches submit browse.html?q=, and Browse restores marine sediment with 3 of 3 matching habitats. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/browse.html?q=marine+sediment">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Record pages</b> <span>present</span>: Every record has a static page at habitats/&lt;label-identifier slug&gt;.html (3,209). Slugs follow the label and identifier, so curation moves URLs, and 739 redirect stubs keep retired addresses resolving. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/marine-sediment-envo-03000033.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Term links</b> <span>partial</span>: Ontology identities on 1,152 records show a resolve link, and broader habitats and related terms are labeled links. But Associated taxa print 12,224 NCBITaxon CURIEs as plain code on 888 record pages, beside a TaxonMech search link that returns 404. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/marine-sediment-envo-03000033.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Evidence links</b> <span>partial</span>: 569 of 3,209 record pages show PMIDs or DOIs as plain text, 2,401 mentions in all: every causal-graph edge reference on the 43 graph pages, such as PMID:31388058 on marine sediment, and identifiers in curation-change text. Only bare URLs in curation text are linked. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/marine-sediment-envo-03000033.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation status</b> <span>present</span>: Every record header shows its grounding status and a workflow status pill, SEEDED on 1,926 records and REVIEWED on 1,283, SEEDED records carry a warning note, and the landing says workflow status does not establish human review. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/a-o-bioreactor-habitatmech-gold-bf15464676.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation history</b> <span>partial</span>: 1,887 record pages list dated, attributed curation events with their change text, such as marine sediment&#x27;s causal-graph events of September 4 and October 12. The seeding event is never rendered, so the 1,322 records whose only event is the seeding, such as 143-B cell, show no dated event. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/143-b-cell-bto-0003167.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Source link</b> <span>present</span>: All 3,209 record pages link View the record to their own data/habitats YAML on GitHub; every target exists in the committed tree and sampled links returned 200. The links follow main rather than the deployed commit. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/marine-sediment-envo-03000033.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Domain visual</b> <span>present</span>: The semantic text map holds 3,209 points with search, click-through to records and ?q= restore. 43 records draw causal-graph SVGs with focusable, labeled nodes and edges, a full-size toggle and an edge-evidence table, such as marine sediment&#x27;s 14 nodes and 14 edges. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/text-map/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Cross-Mech links</b> <span>partial</span>: Every record page now has an Explore across X-Mech block, but all 14,927 TaxonMech and 3,209 TraitMech links target browse.html?q= at each site&#x27;s root and return 404, and the CommunityMech and CultureMech links open site roots. None links a specific sibling record or kg-microbe node. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/agricultural-soil-envo-00002259.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Text embedding map</b> <span>present</span>: The text map shows 3,209 of 3,209 input records with search, and three of three point clicks offered a link to the clicked record. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/text-map/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Open questions</b> <span>not applicable</span>: No committed habitat record carries discussions, and no record page shows any. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/marine-sediment-envo-03000033.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation notice</b> <span>present</span>: The landing and every footer say the corpus is machine-generated, that REVIEWED includes agent-assisted curation and that counts do not establish human review, and SEEDED records carry a note beside the status. REVIEWED headers have no adjacent note and rely on the footer. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/143-b-cell-bto-0003167.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Report a problem</b> <span>missing</span>: Record pages link only their source YAML on GitHub; no edit link, issue link or other route to report a problem is offered. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/marine-sediment-envo-03000033.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Corpus statistics</b> <span>present</span>: The landing shows generated counts: 3,209 habitat records, 2,151 ontology-grounded, 175 corroborated by two or more sources, 1,283 REVIEWED and 683 term requests, with coverage, category and grounding tables. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Category browsing</b> <span>present</span>: The landing Browse by category table and Browse link eight category pages with counts, from HOST_ASSOCIATED (1,655) to CLINICAL (4); 16 paginated listing pages filter and sort the whole category, and record breadcrumbs link the category. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/category/host-associated.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Suite link</b> <span>present</span>: All Mech projects is visible in the header of the landing, Browse, category, record, map, term-request, redirect-stub and 404 pages at 1,280 and 390 px and opens the X-Mech Suite. Only the standalone map source page lacks it. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Breadcrumbs</b> <span>present</span>: Records show a Browse, category and record trail whose links open, and category, term-request and map pages show a Browse trail. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/marine-sediment-envo-03000033.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Not-found page</b> <span>present</span>: Made-up paths at /HabitatMech/, /pages/ and /pages/habitats/ return 404 with the themed No page here page and full navigation, whose links resolve from each path. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/no-such-page-q7.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Downloads</b> <span>partial</span>: The Downloads &amp; Schema section links the YAML directory on GitHub, eight category JSON indexes with six display fields per record, and the map&#x27;s points and manifest; its term_requests.tsv link returns 404. No bulk export of complete records is linked. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/index.html#downloads">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Schema docs</b> <span>partial</span>: The Downloads &amp; Schema section, the footer and each record&#x27;s Provenance now link the LinkML YAML files on GitHub. No readable schema documentation is published or linked. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/index.html#downloads">Evidence</a></li><li><b>License</b> <span>partial</span>: Footers of every templated page state project data CC BY 4.0, code BSD-3-Clause and that third-party terms apply, consistent with the repository. The linked standalone semantic map states no license. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/text-map-source/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Build or version</b> <span>partial</span>: Footers state only Generated from kg-microbe on 2026-08-16, the upstream extraction date, and the map manifest dates its own projection. No page states a build, commit or dated version of HabitatMech&#x27;s own records. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/browse.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Theme toggle</b> <span>partial</span>: A header toggle cycling Auto, Light and Dark carries the choice both ways across the landing, Browse, category, record, map wrapper, term-request, redirect-stub and 404 pages. The linked standalone semantic map ignores it and stays light, so the map shows as a white panel inside the dark wrapper. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/text-map/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Narrow screens</b> <span>partial</span>: A fresh 390 px load of all 3,232 pages found 45 record pages overflowing to 391-558 px, each from a non-wrapping TaxonMech or TraitMech pill in the new Explore across X-Mech block. All other pages fit, and tables scroll in labeled regions. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/sequencing-batch-enhanced-biological-phosphorus-removal-bioreactor-habitatmech-gold-f3de85dd9b.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Load errors</b> <span>partial</span>: The new corpus-wide Browse search hides failures: with the category indexes aborted it shows 0 of 0 matching habitats and stays at zero after the network recovers, and with one index aborted gut silently reports 0 instead of 84. The map wrapper shows a blank frame when its map is aborted, while category pages still say filter unavailable and keep their rows. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/browse.html?q=sediment">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Skip link</b> <span>present</span>: Skip to main content, targeting main, is the first Tab stop on the landing, Browse, record, category, term-request and text-map wrapper pages; only the embedded standalone map page lacks it. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/browse.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Text alternative for visuals</b> <span>partial</span>: Record causal-graph SVGs come with an edge table, 14 rows on marine sediment, but the text map canvas offers only its first 20 search matches. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/text-map/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Works without JavaScript</b> <span>partial</span>: With JavaScript off, record pages render, but category pages show only their first 300 records, such as 300 of 468 on Aquatic, and the search-first Browse page shows no records and no noscript message. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/browse.html">Evidence</a></li></ul>
<h4><a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/">CommunityMech</a></h4><ul><li><b>Search</b> <span>partial</span>: The browser search matches only card names and descriptions: CommunityMech:000134, NCBITaxon:2157 and the file stem ANME_SRB_Marine_Methane_Seep_Consortium each give Showing 0 of 477. Labels work, with ANME giving three openable matches and Geobacter 13, but taxa are found only when a description names them. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/browser.html?q=CommunityMech%3A000134">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Filters</b> <span>present</span>: Category, ecological state, metal, rare-earth and metal-relevance checkboxes combine with search and show per-value counts: SYNTROPHY gives 34, adding STABLE 20 and methane 12, and Clear All restores 477. Active-filter tags remove one filter at a time, and at 390 px a Filters button reveals the panel. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/browser.html?category=SYNTROPHY&amp;state=STABLE">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Result counts</b> <span>present</span>: Showing N of 477 communities updates with search and every facet, through 3 for ANME and 34, 20, 12 and 0 in the facet sequence, and resets to 477. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/browser.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Sorting</b> <span>present</span>: A labeled Sort communities selector offers name A-Z and Z-A, most and fewest members and category, and all five orders over 477 cards had no order violation. The text map, UMAP and graph pages also keep ?q=. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/browser.html?q=methane&amp;category=SYNTROPHY&amp;state=STABLE&amp;sort=members-desc">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Complete catalog</b> <span>present</span>: The browser lists 477 cards with 477 distinct links, equal to the landing total and the 477 committed community records, and all 477 pages returned 200. The seven isolate records are neither counted nor published, and the maps state that they cover subsets. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/browser.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Search from any page</b> <span>present</span>: The landing hero and record-header searches submit browser.html?q=, and the browser restores biomining with 6 of 477 communities shown. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/browser.html?q=biomining">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Record pages</b> <span>present</span>: Every community has a static page at communities/&lt;file stem&gt;.html, and all 477 returned 200. The repository&#x27;s w3id redirects are not live, so the github.io path is the only stable URL. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/communities/ANME_SRB_Marine_Methane_Seep_Consortium.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Term links</b> <span>present</span>: Across 477 pages 5,509 ontology CURIEs are links beside a preferred term and ontology label, including 1,930 ChEBI, 1,781 NCBITaxon, 1,209 GO and 579 ENVO terms; only 11 CURIEs in discussion prose are plain. The record&#x27;s own CommunityMech identifier is plain text on every page. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/communities/ANME_SRB_Marine_Methane_Seep_Consortium.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Evidence links</b> <span>present</span>: Structured evidence is linked, with 6,636 PubMed and 1,121 DOI links carrying support labels and snippets, and on 130 of the 147 pages with a plain-text PMID or DOI the same reference is linked elsewhere on the page. 17 of 477 pages show a reference linked nowhere, 21 identifiers in all, such as doi:10.4014/jmb.2501.01047 in the External Resources of the Apple_Fire_Blight_ANP_SynCom page. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/communities/Apple_Fire_Blight_ANP_SynCom.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation status</b> <span>missing</span>: None of the 477 pages shows a record curation or review status; the header shows the ecological state. The records&#x27; curation history holds review events, but the schema has no record-level status. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/communities/ANME_SRB_Marine_Methane_Seep_Consortium.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation history</b> <span>missing</span>: All 477 records carry curation history, 844 events with timestamp, curator, action and changes, but no page renders a curation-history section. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/communities/Aalborg_East_Full_Scale_EBPR_Community.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Source link</b> <span>present</span>: Every record footer, 477 of 477, links Source YAML: kb/communities/&lt;stem&gt;.yaml on GitHub, and each target exists in the committed tree. The text map (458 points), UMAP (322), graph layout (292) and sequence map (27 proteins) render, filter and open records, though their coverage lags the corpus. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/communities/ANME_SRB_Marine_Methane_Seep_Consortium.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Cross-Mech links</b> <span>partial</span>: 21 pages link 17 specific CultureMech recipes, and all 27 links open. It plots 458 communities while the site counts 477, without saying which are left out. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/text_map.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Open questions</b> <span>partial</span>: Records show Knowledge gaps &amp; discussions with kind and status, such as an open KNOWLEDGE_GAP on Copper Biomining Heap Leach, but the shared discussions index returns 404 and no page links one. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/communities/Copper_Biomining_Heap_Leach.html#discussions">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation notice</b> <span>missing</span>: The landing says only curated, evidence-backed interaction networks; no page says how records were produced, and record pages show no curation status. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Report a problem</b> <span>missing</span>: Record footers link the source YAML and the repository; no edit link, issue link or other route to report a problem is offered. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/communities/Copper_Biomining_Heap_Leach.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Corpus statistics</b> <span>present</span>: The landing shows generated counts of 477 communities and 16 categories, matching the records, and footers repeat 477. There are no standalone category pages or category index. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/browser.html#category-facet">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Suite link</b> <span>present</span>: All Mech projects is visible on the landing, browser, record, text map, UMAP, sequence map, graph layout and schema pages at 1,280 and 390 px. The 404 page lacks it. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Breadcrumbs</b> <span>missing</span>: Record pages have no trail; the header row is the site menu with a Back to Communities link. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/communities/Copper_Biomining_Heap_Leach.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Not-found page</b> <span>partial</span>: Missing paths return 404 with CommunityMech&#x27;s own page, with a search box and nine links; all nine links work at the site root, but under /communities/ all nine relative links and the theme script resolve to 404. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/communities/no-such-page-q7.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Downloads</b> <span>partial</span>: The landing, browser footer and 404 page link communities.json and communities.tsv, 477 rows with only nine summary fields, so taxonomy, interactions, evidence, environment terms and history are absent. <h4><a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/index.html">TaxonMech</a></h4><ul><li><b>Search</b> <span>present</span>: Browse searches all 626,048 records: Methanobrevibacter smithii gives 26 with the exact match first, but identifier search ranks substrings without putting exact matches first, so NCBITaxon:2173 gives 32 with the record second. Matching rows open their record, and an unmatched query gives 0. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/browse.html?q=NCBITaxon%3A2173">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Filters</b> <span>present</span>: Labeled Domain, rank, attesting-source, strain-and-mapping and Status selectors with Reset filters combine: Archaea gives 14,013, adding Species, LPSN and With type strain 733, and Reset restores 626,048. The rank and source selectors omit some values, such as GOLD with 43,822 records, which only text search reaches. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/browse.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Result counts</b> <span>present</span>: A status line, N of 626048 records match with the page number, updates with search, filters and sort, from 32 for NCBITaxon:2173 and 733 for a four-filter view to 0 for no match. Domain and type-strain pages count within their subset. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/browse.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Sorting</b> <span>present</span>: A labeled Sort by selector offers label either way, NCBI ID, rank, BacDive strains, GTDB mappings and attesting sources, and paging through all 3,131 result pages under each order reached all 626,048 records with no violation. Rank sorts alphabetically by rank name rather than by hierarchy, and column headings are not sortable. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/browse.html?sort=strains_desc">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Linkable search</b> <span>partial</span>: The query and sort are written to the URL and restore in a fresh browser and after reload, so ?q=NCBITaxon%3A2173 gives 32 again. The five filters are never written or read: a 185-match Methano view with four filters reopens with 3,045 matches and every filter cleared. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/browse.html?q=Methano&amp;sort=label_desc">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Complete catalog</b> <span>present</span>: All 626,048 records the landing counts are reachable: the 3,131 static browse pages list exactly the index identifiers, dynamic paging reached every record in each sort order, and every record has a detail payload. The 104 type-strain pages list all 20,648 typed taxa. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/browse-3131.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Search from any page</b> <span>present</span>: The landing hero and record-header searches submit browse.html?q=, and Browse restores Methanobrevibacter with 194 of 626,048 records matching. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/browse.html?q=Methanobrevibacter">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Record pages</b> <span>present</span>: Every record has the stable route taxon.html?id=NCBITaxon:N, with a payload for all 626,048, and 63,699 legacy URLs redirect there. Records are rendered in the browser, and invalid identifiers show explicit alerts. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/taxon.html?id=NCBITaxon:2173">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Term links</b> <span>present</span>: The record&#x27;s own NCBITaxon CURIE links to NCBI on all 626,048 records, lineage labels link to local records or NCBI, and mapping and attestation CURIEs are linked beside their labels. Matched-deposit kgmicrobe.strain codes and some strain assembly codes are plain, and the causal-graph tables on 15 records omit node groundings. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/taxon.html?id=NCBITaxon:2173">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Evidence links</b> <span>present</span>: A scan of every rendered record found 402,247 DOI links and 20,697 PubMed links, with no plain-text URL or bare DOI outside links. The exception is 40 PMIDs in curation-history text on nine causal-graph records, such as E. coli. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/taxon.html?id=NCBITaxon:511145">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation status</b> <span>present</span>: Every record header, Browse row and the Status filter show the mapping status, SEEDED for 626,034 records and DEPRECATED for 14, such as Anaeromusa acidaminophila, alongside the grounding status. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/taxon.html?id=NCBITaxon:81464">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation history</b> <span>present</span>: All 626,048 records list dated events as timestamp, curator and action with a change summary, 626,087 events in all, such as M. smithii&#x27;s 2026-09-13 seeding from its attesting sources. The 15 causal-graph records list two to five events with an LLM-assisted flag. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/taxon.html?id=NCBITaxon:562">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Source link</b> <span>present</span>: Every record, 626,048 of 626,048, links its own data/taxa/&lt;domain&gt;/&lt;slug&gt;.yaml on GitHub, every linked path exists in the committed tree, and sampled links returned 200. The links follow main rather than the deployed commit. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/taxon.html?id=NCBITaxon:2173">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Domain visual</b> <span>present</span>: All 626,048 records show a labeled, linked root-to-parent lineage and a child-taxa list, 3,175 linked children for E. coli, and a sampled child link opened its record. The new text map, which never renders, is judged under Text embedding map. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/taxon.html?id=NCBITaxon:562">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Cross-Mech links</b> <span>partial</span>: The five new Explore across X-Mech search links on every record are broken: the CultureMech, HabitatMech, PathwayMech and NaturalProductMech links return 404, and CommunityMech&#x27;s search shows 0 of 477 because it does not index NCBITaxon identifiers. None links a specific record, and kg-microbe strain identifiers remain plain text. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/taxon.html?id=NCBITaxon:562">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Text embedding map</b> <span>missing</span>: The text map never renders: its script throws a TypeError and the canvas stays empty, so no point can be clicked. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/map.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Open questions</b> <span>not applicable</span>: No committed taxon record carries discussions, and no record page shows any. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/taxon.html?id=NCBITaxon:562">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation notice</b> <span>partial</span>: The landing says records are seeded from committed kg-microbe inventories, but record headers show SEEDED with no explanation, and no page says what the status means. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/taxon.html?id=NCBITaxon:562">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Report a problem</b> <span>missing</span>: Record pages link their own YAML on GitHub but offer no edit link, issue link or other route to report a problem. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/taxon.html?id=NCBITaxon:562">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Corpus statistics</b> <span>present</span>: The landing shows generated counts of 626,048 taxon records, 100,745 distinct listed strains and 20,648 with a type strain, and its Domains table sums to the total and matches the listing pages. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Category browsing</b> <span>present</span>: The landing&#x27;s By domain card and Domains table link the four domain listings, from Bacteria (3,029 static pages) to Other (1), and the type-strain stat links that 104-page subset, each searchable and filterable within it. On domain pages the Domain selector still offers other domains, which give 0. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/domain/archaea.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Suite link</b> <span>present</span>: All Mech projects is in the header and footer and is visible on the landing, Browse, type-strain, domain, Sources, map, record and record-error pages at 1,280 and 390 px, opening the X-Mech Suite. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Breadcrumbs</b> <span>partial</span>: Records show a TaxonMech › Browse › Taxon record trail linking home and Browse, but not the domain page, and the four sampled lineage parents link to NCBI Taxonomy rather than TaxonMech pages. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/taxon.html?id=NCBITaxon:2173">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Not-found page</b> <span>missing</span>: Made-up paths at /TaxonMech/, /pages/ and /pages/taxa/archaea/ return 404 with GitHub&#x27;s generic File not found page. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/no-such-page-q7.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Downloads</b> <span>partial</span>: index.json.gz, 8 MB with an integrity manifest, is now linked from the navigation, landing, Sources and every footer, but it is the Browse summary index rather than complete records, although the landing calls it the complete harmonized taxon index. Full records exist only as per-record YAML on GitHub. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Schema docs</b> <span>partial</span>: The Schema navigation item, the landing&#x27;s LinkML schema button and every footer now link the schema YAML on GitHub instead of the repository root. No readable schema documentation is published. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>License</b> <span>present</span>: Every page type&#x27;s footer states project-authored data CC BY 4.0, code BSD-3-Clause and that upstream data keep their source terms, linking LICENSE-DATA and LICENSE-CODE, consistent with the repository. Sources adds attribution licences for AllTheBacteria and StrainInfo. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Build or version</b> <span>partial</span>: Footers now state the generator with a corpus digest and the 626,048-record count but give no date, build time or commit. Sources gives upstream snapshot dates and a source-audit date. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Theme toggle</b> <span>present</span>: The toggle persists the choice in both directions across the landing, two Browse pages, type strains, a domain page, Sources, the map, a record, a legacy redirect and a record-error page, and switching on a record carries back to the landing. Enter toggles it from the keyboard. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Narrow screens</b> <span>partial</span>: At 390 px the landing, Browse, type-strain and domain listings, Sources, map and error states fit, and wide tables scroll in labeled regions. But 22 of 413 sampled records overflow, up to 928 px, from long unbroken taxon names in the heading, and measuring the 169,495 labels with a token of 14 or more characters puts 7,795 of 626,048 record headings wider than the content box. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/taxon.html?id=NCBITaxon:2014251">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Load errors</b> <span>partial</span>: Aborting index.json.gz on Browse or a domain page keeps static pagination with a message that search is unavailable, and an aborted record shard shows an alert with Try again that recovers. But the selector labeled Pathway class lists 105 edge-count strings, such as 10 mechanistic edges, rather than pathway classes, and Curated mechanistic edges matches every record. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/browse.html?taxon=Saccharomyces+cerevisiae+S288C&amp;grounding=protein-grounded">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Result counts</b> <span>present</span>: The live match count updates with search, every filter and sort, through 1, 2, 109, 85 and 0 of 274, and resets to 274. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/browse.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Sorting</b> <span>present</span>: A labeled Sort pathways selector offers name either way, mechanistic edges high or low, identifier and Pathway class, and every order held over 274 rows, combined with filters and kept in ?sort=. The Pathway class order sorts edge-count strings lexically, putting 10 and 100 before 2. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/browse.html?sort=edges-desc">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Linkable search</b> <span>present</span>: The query, type, taxon, grounding and sort are written to the URL, and a coli, protein-grounded, most-edges view restores all three controls and 106 ordered results in a fresh browser and after reload. The text map restores ?q= too. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/browse.html?q=coli&amp;grounding=protein-grounded&amp;sort=edges-desc">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Complete catalog</b> <span>present</span>: Browse lists 274 rows with 274 distinct record links, equal to the landing total, the download manifest and the 274 committed record pages, and all 274 returned 200. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/browse.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Search from any page</b> <span>present</span>: The landing hero and record top-bar searches submit browse.html?q=, and Browse restores glycolysis with 1 of 274 pathways matching. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/browse.html?q=glycolysis">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Record pages</b> <span>present</span>: Every record has a static page at records/&lt;identifier slug&gt;.html, and all 274 returned 200 and match the committed pages. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/records/MetaCyc_PWY-6543.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Term links</b> <span>partial</span>: Terms are labeled, but none of the 9,787 component rows or 29,198 mechanistic-edge endpoints on the 274 pages is a link, and the record&#x27;s own identifier is plain text on every page. Only the organism-scope taxa link to NCBI Taxonomy. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/records/MetaCyc_PWY-6543.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Evidence links</b> <span>partial</span>: 201 of 275 record pages show a reference linked nowhere: Rhea, identifiers.org and OBO IRIs printed as text in mechanistic-edge source locations, not counting Rhea locators beside a linked Rhea entry, and on 38 pages a PMID or DOI, 28 of them in prose, as on GO_0019618. Only 1 of the 7,082 References entries is unlinked. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/records/GO_0019618.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation status</b> <span>missing</span>: No record page shows a record-level curation or review status; the header shows pathway type and organism scope. The schema has no status slot, although 207 structured reviews name their target records. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/records/MetaCyc_PWY-6543.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation history</b> <span>present</span>: All 274 pages have a Curation history section with dated entries, 828 in all, giving curator, action and changes, plus recorded sessions with typed events and links to read each session. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/records/GO_0036040.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Source link</b> <span>present</span>: Read the source YAML on all 274 pages links the record&#x27;s own data/pathways file on GitHub, and every target exists in the committed tree. The links follow main. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/records/MetaCyc_PWY-6543.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Domain visual</b> <span>present</span>: Pathway networks render on all 274 pages as zoomable SVGs with Find a node highlighting, and clicking an arrow or pressing Enter on an edge link opens its evidence row; without JavaScript the static SVG and edge links remain. The two embedding maps render and filter, but their point clicks open 404 pages. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/records/MetaCyc_PWY-6543.html#mechanism-edge-1">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Cross-Mech links</b> <span>missing</span>: None of the 274 record pages links a specific sibling-Mech or kg-microbe record, although TraitMech links into 11 PathwayMech records. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/records/MetaCyc_PWY-6543.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Text embedding map</b> <span>partial</span>: The text map renders 270 of 274 records with filters and search, but three of three point clicks went to record addresses that return 404, because they use a slug rather than the records/&lt;Source&gt;_&lt;id&gt;.html names the record pages have. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/map.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Open questions</b> <span>not applicable</span>: The schema has no discussions slot, and no committed pathway record carries discussions. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/records/MetaCyc_PWY-6543.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation notice</b> <span>missing</span>: No page says how records were produced, and record pages show no curation status. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Report a problem</b> <span>missing</span>: Record pages link their YAML file on GitHub and their curation-session files; no edit link, issue link or other route to report a problem is offered. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/records/MetaCyc_PWY-6543.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Corpus statistics</b> <span>present</span>: The generated landing reports 274 pathway records, 105 taxa, 14,599 mechanistic edges and 5,911 distinct references, matching the download manifest&#x27;s 274 records and 14,599 edge rows. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Category browsing</b> <span>partial</span>: Subsets are reachable only as filters: Browse filters by organism and grounding, and the text map by the 12 pathway classes. There are no category or class pages, and Browse&#x27;s Pathway class values are edge counts. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/browse.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Suite link</b> <span>present</span>: All Mech projects is visible in the navigation of the landing, Browse, record and both map pages at 1,280 and 390 px, and the landing footer repeats it. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Breadcrumbs</b> <span>present</span>: Records show a PathwayMech / Browse pathways / identifier trail whose links open; the site has no category pages. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/records/MetaCyc_PWY-6543.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Not-found page</b> <span>missing</span>: Made-up paths at /PathwayMech/, /pages/ and /pages/records/ return 404 with GitHub&#x27;s generic File not found page. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/records/no-such-page-q7.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Downloads</b> <span>present</span>: The landing and the Downloads &amp; Schema navigation item link full KGX nodes.tsv (5,446 rows) and edges.tsv (14,599 rows with per-edge evidence), pathway_index.json for all 274 records and manifest.json, whose SHA-256 values and row counts match both TSV files. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/index.html#downloads-title">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Schema docs</b> <span>partial</span>: The landing&#x27;s downloads and schema section, reached from every page&#x27;s navigation, now links the LinkML YAML files and source configuration on GitHub. There are no static per-record pages. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/browse.html#record=MCSA%3A1">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Term links</b> <span>partial</span>: Cross-references, parents, examples, chemistry and evidence lists are labeled links where a resolver exists, but 254,686 of 429,377 records (59.3%) show Source entry link unavailable for their own identifier, including all ECOD, NCBIfam and CDD records. Some CATH and all 370 MEROPS family links open error pages, and causal-graph node groundings are plain text. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/browse.html#record=ECOD%3AF.3755.3.1.364">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Evidence links</b> <span>partial</span>: The Evidence list links its PubMed and DOI references, but the Causal graphs section prints every edge&#x27;s evidence as plain text: 726 PMIDs and URLs in 13 of the 17 graph-bearing sampled records, 200 on MCSA:1 alone. Evidence notes also repeat plain PMIDs beside the linked reference, as on RHEA:83619. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/browse.html#record=MCSA%3A1">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation status</b> <span>present</span>: Every record view shows a status pill and a Status row, and the Status facet filters on it; corpus totals are SEEDED 388,664, REVIEWED 39,591 and PROPOSED 1,122. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/browse.html#record=MCSA%3A1">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation history</b> <span>present</span>: Records with curation history show each event&#x27;s timestamp, curator, action and an LLM-assisted flag, as all 17 sampled REVIEWED records did, such as MCSA:1&#x27;s 2026-07-29 causal-graph addition. Seeded records without history omit the row. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/browse.html#record=ARO%3A3002686">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Source link</b> <span>present</span>: Repository YAML source links the record&#x27;s own file on GitHub on all 53 sampled and four cold-loaded records, and the builder writes a path for every record. The link follows main rather than the deployed commit. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/browse.html#record=Pfam%3APF23751">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Domain visual</b> <span>present</span>: Example proteins show a sequence viewer with per-residue feature strips, and map.html has four modes: full-record and definition maps of 344,797 records with a coverage note, 68,657 proteins and 12,705 sequences. Each has a category filter, search, legend toggles, URL state, a CSV download and click-through to the record or UniProt entry. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/map.html#sequences">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Cross-Mech links</b> <span>missing</span>: Record views link no specific sibling-Mech or kg-microbe record: none of the 53 sampled records has a sibling link, and sibling sites are linked only from footers. The repository&#x27;s cross-Mech protein inventory is not used by the site. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/browse.html#record=METPO%3A1000639">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Text embedding map</b> <span>present</span>: The map states that 344,797 of 429,377 current records are plotted and 84,580 fall outside its embedding snapshot, and three of three point clicks opened the clicked record&#x27;s view. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/map.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Open questions</b> <span>not applicable</span>: No committed trait record carries discussions, and no record view shows any. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/browse.html#record=CDD%3Acd05137">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation notice</b> <span>partial</span>: The landing&#x27;s curation model says seeded records start as SEEDED and curator review makes them REVIEWED, but record views show the status pill without an explanation. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Report a problem</b> <span>missing</span>: Record views link only their source YAML on GitHub; no edit link, issue link or other route to report a problem is offered. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/browse.html#record=CDD%3Acd05137">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Corpus statistics</b> <span>present</span>: The landing shows counts rendered in CI: 429,377 trait records, per-axis counts and category and source tables. The landing, map and SLC10 search placeholders hard-code 277,953 traits, contradicting that total. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Category browsing</b> <span>partial</span>: The landing&#x27;s axis, category and source tables link Browse hash filters, and a cold #cat=STRUCT_ACTIVE_SITE route restores 1,004 records. There are no category pages and no category index beyond that table. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Suite link</b> <span>present</span>: All Mech projects is in the landing navigation, the Browse navigation including record views, the map header, the SLC10 navigation and the footers of the schema, example and biophysical pages. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Breadcrumbs</b> <span>partial</span>: Record views show only a back to results link; there is no category or parent link. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/browse.html#record=CDD%3Acd05137">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Not-found page</b> <span>missing</span>: Made-up paths at /proteintraitsmech/ and /proteintraitsmech/records/ return 404 with GitHub&#x27;s generic File not found page. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/no-such-page-q7.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Downloads</b> <span>partial</span>: Browse&#x27;s only data link is data/facets.json, labeled record-shard manifest and facet counts; the maps offer CSVs of plotted points and SLC10 its evidence JSON. The landing links no data, and no bulk export of complete records exists. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/browse.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Schema docs</b> <span>present</span>: schema.html describes ProteinTraitRecord, the enums and causal graphs and links the LinkML YAML, and Browse, the landing and the Jekyll navigation link it. It is hand-written, last edited on 2026-09-24 before the 2026-10-14 schema change, and names 11 of the schema&#x27;s 69 classes. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/schema.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>License</b> <span>partial</span>: The landing, Browse and record views, maps and Jekyll pages state project data CC BY 4.0 and code BSD-3-Clause with third-party terms, consistent with the license files. The SLC10 pilot, linked from the navigation, says only that source licences remain attached to their artifacts. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/slc10.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Build or version</b> <span>partial</span>: No page states a build date, commit or dated corpus version. Pages show only upstream releases and snapshots: the UniProt release on examples, the map&#x27;s embedding-snapshot note and the SLC10 bundle version. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Theme toggle</b> <span>partial</span>: The injected toggle carries a choice from a record view to Browse, both map modes, the schema, example and biophysical pages and the landing. But the landing&#x27;s own header toggle does nothing when clicked, and SLC10 has no toggle and ignores the saved choice. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Narrow screens</b> <span>partial</span>: Fresh 390 px loads fit for 13 pages and all 53 sampled record views, and seven pages narrowed from 1,280 px also fit. Expanding the Category or Status facet group still widens Browse to 400 px. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/browse.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Load errors</b> <span>present</span>: Every injected failure shows an explicit error, and every Retry offered recovers: an aborted facets.json says Failed to load index, an aborted shard or lookup bucket says Records could not be loaded, aborted map data says This map could not be loaded, and an aborted SLC10 bundle says Cannot display verified evidence. The map-coverage failure shows a message without Retry. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/browse.html#record=MCSA%3A1">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Skip link</b> <span>missing</span>: The first Tab stop on the landing, Browse, record views, SLC10 and map pages is the logo or home link; no page has a skip link. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/browse.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Text alternative for visuals</b> <span>missing</span>: The corpus, protein and sequence maps are canvases that offer only a CSV download of their points, with no on-page table or list. The sequence-feature viewer on example proteins was not assessed. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/map.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Works without JavaScript</b> <span>missing</span>: With JavaScript off, Browse and record views show only Loading records…, with no heading, record content or noscript message; only the landing renders. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/browse.html">Evidence</a></li></ul>
<h4><a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/">DUFMech</a></h4><ul><li><b>Search</b> <span>present</span>: The labeled search covers all 8,296 families: PF04149, its label Domain of unknown function (DUF397), IPR007278 and PF18141 each give one openable match, and an unmatched query shows an explicit empty state. A family-page header search for PF18141 opened the catalogue with the query applied. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/index.html?q=PF04149#families">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Filters</b> <span>present</span>: Seed status, Characterization and Curation status selects combine with search and reset: EX_DUF gives 1,796, EX_DUF + IN_PROGRESS 127 and adding DUF 4, and Clear search and filters restores 8,296. Characterization has one value, UNSCORED, so it cannot narrow results. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/index.html?seed=EX_DUF&amp;curation=IN_PROGRESS#families">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Result counts</b> <span>present</span>: The live match count updates with every search, filter and sort; the static page shows 8,296 of 8,296 families on page 1 of 166, and category pages count against the corpus, such as 1,796 of 8,296. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/index.html#families">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Sorting</b> <span>present</span>: Five orders, by protein count either way, Pfam accession, family name and structures, each matched an independently computed order on the first and last of 166 pages, so sorting applies before paging; the choice is kept in ?sort=. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/index.html?sort=name-asc#families">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Linkable search</b> <span>present</span>: The query, filters, sort and page are written to the query string, and a DUF search of UNKNOWN_CANDIDATE families in Pfam order on page 2 restores every control and the same 50 rows in a fresh browser and after reload. Both maps restore their seed filter and colour from the URL. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/index.html?q=DUF&amp;seed=UNKNOWN_CANDIDATE&amp;sort=pfam_id-asc&amp;page=2#families">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Complete catalog</b> <span>present</span>: The 166 static listing pages, which need no JavaScript, link exactly the 8,296 families in the catalogue, equal to the 8,296 deployed family routes and the landing total, and the category subsets paginate the same way. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/browse/166.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Search from any page</b> <span>present</span>: The landing hero search filters the in-page catalogue, giving 1 of 8,296 for PF04149, and every family-page header search submits index.html?q=...#families, which restores and applies the query. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/index.html?q=PF04149#families">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Record pages</b> <span>present</span>: Every family has a static page at families/PFxxxxx.html, all 8,296 listed in the deployed manifest, and all 344 sampled pages returned 200 and match the committed pages. Each also offers its JSON and, for 8,290 families, a FASTA file. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF04149.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Term links</b> <span>partial</span>: Own grounding is labeled and linked on all 344 sampled pages: Pfam on every one and InterPro on the 343 families that have an InterPro entry. But the causal-graph node lists added to 134 families print 1,770 grounding CURIEs, mostly UniProtKB, as plain text, as on PF00563. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF00563.html#causal-graphs">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Evidence links</b> <span>partial</span>: Structured references and causal-graph citations are linked, but 16 of the 141 curated family pages show a PMID linked nowhere on the page, in curation-history or discussion text, such as the three PMIDs in PF01062&#x27;s discussions. One seeded family, PF10350, also prints a preprint DOI in its source description. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF01062.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation status</b> <span>present</span>: Every family page shows Curation status, SEEDED for 8,155 families and IN_PROGRESS for 141, separately from seed status and characterization, and the catalogue has a Curation status column and filter. The status guide explains that SEEDED is not expert review. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF00563.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation history</b> <span>present</span>: 141 families list dated events with agent, action, summary and a linked history file, and 142 link retained reviews with their scope; families without events say so, as 203 of 344 sampled pages do. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF00563.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Source link</b> <span>present</span>: All 344 sampled pages link their own data/families YAML on GitHub at corpus commit 4c55df70, plus a build-local YAML copy and the family JSON. Every family file is identical at that commit and at the deployed main. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF04149.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Domain visual</b> <span>present</span>: Two interactive maps, a text map of all 8,296 families and an ESM-2 sequence map of 634 proteins, offer hover, click-through, colour-by, filters, URL state and a coordinates download. Records add domain match tracks and causal-graph edge tables, and the landing a family-size histogram. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/map.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Cross-Mech links</b> <span>present</span>: 176 of 344 sampled family pages list 486 Related Mech records rows, each linking a specific ProteinTraitsMech record file on GitHub at a pinned commit, and sampled links opened. Families added after the scan say they were not covered. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF01519.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Text embedding map</b> <span>present</span>: The text map shows 8,296 of 8,296 families with filters and a legend, and three of three point clicks opened a panel linking the clicked family. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/map.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Open questions</b> <span>partial</span>: Family pages show discussions with kind and status, such as PF00563&#x27;s open CURATION_TODO, but no discussions index is published or linked. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF00563.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation notice</b> <span>present</span>: A Status and evidence guide on the landing and family pages explains seed status, characterization and curation status, including that SEEDED is source-derived rather than expert-reviewed, beside each family&#x27;s status fields. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF00563.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Report a problem</b> <span>missing</span>: Family pages link curation-history files and a site-served YAML copy; no edit link, issue link or other route to report a problem is offered. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF04149.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Corpus statistics</b> <span>present</span>: The generated landing shows 8,296 families, 8,238 with InterPro IDs, 1,906 with structures, 8,242 with AlphaFold models and per-family protein and match totals, with caveats for missing counts. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Category browsing</b> <span>present</span>: The linked Categories page lists EX DUF (1,796), KNOWN HISTORICAL DUF (373) and UNKNOWN CANDIDATE (6,127) families, and each subset has static paginated pages with search, filters and sort scoped to the category. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/categories.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Suite link</b> <span>present</span>: All Mech projects is in the header of every page type: the landing, browse pages, categories, category subsets, all 344 sampled family pages, sources, schema, cross-Mech and both maps. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Breadcrumbs</b> <span>partial</span>: Family pages show an All families / Pfam identifier trail linking the catalogue, but not the family&#x27;s category, although category pages exist. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF04149.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Not-found page</b> <span>missing</span>: Made-up paths at /DUFMech/ and /DUFMech/families/ return 404 with GitHub&#x27;s generic File not found page. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/no-such-page-q7.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Downloads</b> <span>present</span>: Every catalogue page and Sources &amp; schema link the complete family index, index.json at 24.6 MB, which holds all 8,296 families with full record metadata and matches the committed copy. Per-family JSON and FASTA, map coordinates and frozen datasets with SHA-256 manifests supplement it. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/index.json">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Schema docs</b> <span>present</span>: schema.html is generated from the LinkML schema at build time, with field, range, requirement, description and pattern tables for each class, enumerations and a downloadable schema; Sources &amp; schema links it. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/schema.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>License</b> <span>present</span>: Every footer states project data CC BY 4.0, code BSD 3-Clause and that redistributed source material keeps its own terms, linking the license policy, consistent with the repository&#x27;s license files. The data link opens the Creative Commons deed rather than LICENSE-DATA. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/sources.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Build or version</b> <span>present</span>: Every footer states a site build fingerprint, the linked corpus source commit 4c55df70 and dated input snapshot identifiers such as the 2026-10-10 worklist. The deployment commit is not shown. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Theme toggle</b> <span>present</span>: A labeled System, Light and Dark selector persists the choice: dark chosen on the landing and light chosen on a family page each carried across 12 further page types, and System follows the operating system. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Narrow screens</b> <span>present</span>: Fresh 390 px loads of 359 pages, including 344 family pages and the first and last page of each category, found no overflow, and eight pages loaded at 1,280 px and narrowed to 390 px also fit. Wide tables scroll inside focusable regions. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF00563.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Load errors</b> <span>present</span>: Aborting catalogue.json says search is unavailable and its parameters were not applied, with a Retry that recovers, while the 50 static rows and numbered pages remain. Aborting either map&#x27;s data shows an alert with a working Retry. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/index.html?q=PF04149#families">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Skip link</b> <span>present</span>: Skip to content, targeting main, is the first Tab stop on the landing, family, category, static browse, both map and categories pages. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF04149.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Text alternative for visuals</b> <span>partial</span>: The landing family-size histogram and record domain-match tracks are presented as labeled text, but the text map and sequence map canvases have no table or list alternative. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/map.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Works without JavaScript</b> <span>present</span>: With JavaScript off, a noscript note points to the 166 static catalogue pages, which reach every family, and family pages render. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/browse/2.html">Evidence</a></li></ul>
<h4><a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/NaturalProductMech/pages/index.html">NaturalProductMech</a></h4><ul><li><b>Search</b> <span>present</span>: Browse searches all 3,116 records by own identifier, InChIKey, cross-reference, source id, label and synonym: CHEBI:338412, YKJYKKNCCRKFSL-RDBSUJKOSA-N, npatlas:NPA017379, BGC0001510, marneral and tetrabromopyrrole each give one match that opens, and mycin gives 535. Producers are not indexed, so Streptomyces returns 0 although the landing promises producer search. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/NaturalProductMech/pages/browse.html?q=CHEBI%3A338412">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Filters</b> <span>present</span>: Browse adds filing-pathway and producer-evidence selects and gene-cluster, molecular-target and bioactivity checkboxes with Reset: terpenoids gives 213, causal producer 785, and mycin + polyketides + causal + gene cluster 56. Pathway pages offer their own evidence and occurrence filters; the gene-cluster box cannot narrow, since every record carries a cluster. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/NaturalProductMech/pages/browse.html?q=mycin&amp;pathway=polyketides&amp;claim=causal&amp;bgc=1">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Result counts</b> <span>present</span>: The live match count updates with every search and filter, such as 535 of 3,116 for mycin and 0 with an empty message; pathway pages say Showing 64 of 213 compounds, and the maps report their own counts. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/NaturalProductMech/pages/browse.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Sorting</b> <span>present</span>: Sort compounds by offers name either way, causal producers, gene clusters, bioactivities and identifier, and every order held over all adjacent pairs, sorting records within each filing-pathway group. Pathway pages sort their table six ways, and the sort is kept in the URL. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/NaturalProductMech/pages/browse.html?sort=bioactivity-desc">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Linkable search</b> <span>present</span>: Browse writes the query, pathway, evidence, cluster, target, bioactivity and sort to the URL, and a mycin, polyketides, causal, gene-cluster, Z-A view restores every control and 56 of 3,116 in a fresh browser and after reload. Pathway pages and all three maps also restore their state from the URL. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/NaturalProductMech/pages/browse.html?q=mycin&amp;pathway=polyketides&amp;claim=causal&amp;bgc=1&amp;sort=label-desc">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Complete catalog</b> <span>present</span>: Browse links exactly the 3,116 committed record pages, matching the footer total, the eight pathway indexes list 3,116 rows, and all returned 200 at 390 px. The text map states that it covers 3,114 compounds. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/NaturalProductMech/pages/browse.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Search from any page</b> <span>present</span>: The landing hero and record top-bar searches submit browse.html?q=, and Browse restores marneral with 1 of 3,116 compounds matching. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/NaturalProductMech/pages/browse.html?q=marneral">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Record pages</b> <span>present</span>: Each record has a static page at &lt;pathway&gt;/&lt;yaml stem&gt;.html, and all 3,116 returned 200. URLs follow the filing pathway, so a re-filed record moves, and no redirect stubs exist. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/NaturalProductMech/pages/alkaloids/anisomycin.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Term links</b> <span>partial</span>: No CURIE on any of the 3,116 record pages links a resolver: own CHEBI and minted identifiers, producer and host NCBITaxon IDs, MIBiG and GenBank accessions, cross-references and the UniProtKB, RHEA and EC terms in biosynthesis and graph tables are plain text. Taxa and graph nodes carry readable labels. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/NaturalProductMech/pages/alkaloids/anisomycin.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Evidence links</b> <span>partial</span>: 3,090 of 3,116 record pages show PMID, DOI or URL references as plain text, and record pages have no PubMed or DOI anchors, across occurrence references, producer evidence, causal-graph edges and bioactivities; anisomycin&#x27;s PMID:28373542 is an example. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/NaturalProductMech/pages/alkaloids/anisomycin.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation status</b> <span>present</span>: Every record header shows grounding (EXACT 364, MINTED 2,743, REVIEW_NEEDED 9) and curation status (all 3,116 SEEDED) tags, with a What these status tags mean disclosure. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/NaturalProductMech/pages/alkaloids/anisomycin.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation history</b> <span>missing</span>: All 3,116 records carry curation history, 318 with events beyond seeding, but no record page renders it; the only pointer is a GitHub commit-history link inside the status-tag disclosure. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/NaturalProductMech/pages/polyketides/rabelomycin.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Source link</b> <span>present</span>: All 3,116 record pages link their own data/natural_products/&lt;pathway&gt;/&lt;slug&gt;.yaml on GitHub, and every target exists in the committed tree. The links follow main. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/NaturalProductMech/pages/alkaloids/anisomycin.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Domain visual</b> <span>present</span>: The structure map draws a UMAP of chiral Morgan fingerprints for all 3,116 compounds with search, pathway filter, colour modes, structural neighbours and ?id= deep links, the corpus text map draws 3,114 points with click-through, and the sequence map shows 763 proteins. ## How the Mechs reference each other
{: .fleet-section-target}

Beyond shared vocabulary, Mechs name one another directly, in record fields, in schema slots, and in the curation practices they adopt from each other. The graph draws biological data links and complementary coverage; this detailed list also includes shared software and record formats. Arrows point at the Mech that consumes, or at the one a scope decision defers to. Taxa tie TaxonMech to much of the fleet through shared NCBI Taxonomy identifiers, while DUFMech connects to ProteinTraitsMech records through shared Pfam and InterPro families. Those shared identifiers appear as chords in the graph's vocabulary layer. DUFMech also retains a frozen evidence snapshot from seven sibling Mechs, with source commits and explicit link bases; those imports appear as directed data links. Direct-reference arrows require a committed record, schema or curation practice naming the other Mech.

Its curation-event helper and closed-schema write gate are adapted from HabitatMech's copies; their docstrings still credit TraitMech, which HabitatMech ported them from.<small>Practice · AntibioticMech scripts/render_pages.py; src/antibioticmech/templates/style.css; src/antibioticmech/curate/curation_event.py</small></div></div>
